# SP40_CW_81 — consolidated reading decisions

Every reading rests on the supplied scan. Three independent proposed readings are retained under `readers/`; main-reader decisions are retained under `records/`. Rows resolving or retaining a C doubt are included even when the final reading has no textual change. Counts refer to decision loci, not changed tokens; macro syntax alone is excluded. H has no collation rows on scans with no transcribed text.

## Consequential corrections

- Scan 003, Polynomial block: coefficient expression: The written expression is C(z); the transcription retains those round delimiters beside the word polynômes.
- Scan 003, Question insertion: attachment: The equality P'(z)=Q'(z) qualifies the author's question at the insertion guide; H attaches it to the preceding conclusion.
- Scan 003, Last block: consequence insertion position: The inserted consequence comment follows deg P≥1 in the manuscript; H places it before P(0)=0.
- Scan 003, Last block: pour P(z)≠0 insertion: The nonzero restriction P(z)≠0 is attached to the final question before P'(z)=0; H places it before P₀(1)=1.
- Scan 003, Sideways note: two zeros: The sideways calculation includes the comparison of −b/(2a) with the two stacked zeros 0 and −b/a, omitted from H.
- Scan 003, Sideways note: final condition: The sideways continuation states si b²≠−4a; H leaves this final condition unread.
- Scan 004, Right lower system, even-branch eta subscript: The lower even-case eta factor reads i−1; H supplies i+1 and the following written result retains i+2. Root's crop reading governs the index.
- Scan 004, Expansion, first f in first factor: The first factor of the expansion contains f_i; the edit from H replaces its first subscript 1 by i.
- Scan 004, Expansion, e in first factor: The first e index remains explicitly i/1. C gives a firm i and H a firm 1; the final formula retains both candidates at this locus.
- Scan 004, Expansion RHS, first product first factor: In the first right-hand product, the edit from H changes f_1 f_1 to f_i f_1, preserving the second subscript 1.
- Scan 004, Expansion RHS, lambda-squared product first factor: In the lambda-squared product, the edit from H changes e_1 e_1 to e_i e_1, preserving the second subscript 1.
- Scan 004, Expansion RHS, first cross term first factor: In the first cross term, the edit from H changes f_1 e_1 to f_i e_1.
- Scan 004, Expansion RHS, second cross term first factor: In the second cross term, the edit from H changes e_1 f_1 to e_i f_1; the product also retains its local cancellation.
- Scan 006, U4, superscript on the product: The proved morphism in checks/scan006_algebra.md sends x and y to their stated second-order epsilon polynomials. Event 006-second-product extracts twice the epsilon² coefficient of their product and obtains x2*y0+2*x1*y1+x0*y2. The written 2/double-prime alternatives remain; the coefficient calculation does not convert the 2-like superscript into certain derivative notation.
- Scan 006, U6, full expanded-fraction context; local sign, factor-state and exponent decisions separately mapped: The intermediate numerator is reproduced in full, including both struck P'Q' products and the separately struck final Q. The four existing events verify their stated jet and final quotient/product coefficient expressions; they assign no chronology to these local cancellations.
- Scan 006, U6, Q-squared immediately after the first closing parenthesis: The base Q is live. The alternatives concern a cancelled or live raised 2, preserving the single-Q and Q² layer states within the same factor locus. The displayed intermediate fraction undergoes no cancellation or power reduction; chronology remains unassigned.
- Scan 006, U6, denominator exponent: The denominator is Q³. The existing second-quotient certificate uses Q0³ beneath P2*Q0²−Q2*P0*Q0−2*P1*Q0*Q1+2*P0*Q1². The explicit generator map and verified inverse relation are in checks/scan006_algebra.md; the 3 glyph is resolved from the crop.
- Scan 006, U8, first squared letter: The fragment begins with lowercase p² followed by Q². The quotient certificate uses P-labelled generators; the fragment supplies no identification p=P, so lowercase p is retained.
- Scan 006, U8, minus before Q-prime/Q: The separate minus precedes Q'/Q within the open parenthesis. The fragment still ends with its final plus and no closing continuation.
- Scan 006, U8, complete unfinished lowest fragment: The written expression remains 2p²Q²(−Q'/Q+ with no supplied next summand or completed identity.
- Scan 006, R1, relation after the determinant matrix: The determinant row retains the single dash before ad−bc and the following inversible. No equals sign is inserted, and none of the four recorded coefficient events verifies a determinant assertion.
- Scan 006, R2, locally cancelled ending of the upper line: The =e_d ending remains locally cancelled within the globally cancelled layer. The index d and local deletion are preserved without appending that expression to the surviving row.
- Scan 006, R3, overwritten f before e1: The cancelled f is retained beneath live e₁. The surviving product remains e₁·f_{d−3}, including the explicit multiplication dot.
- Scan 006, R5, second listed product and its overwritten first letter: The second product e₁f_{d−3} is retained among the four written products, together with its cancelled predecessor f. H omits this product. The prose’s linear-combination assertion is transcribed; the four checks do not concern the delta systems.
- Scan 006, R5, index on the second basis letter after des: The prose names e_{d−1} and f_{d−2}; H’s second basis index is d−1. The record preserves the changed second indexed symbol and supplies no identification between those indexed elements. The four coefficient checks concern the upright quotient/product expressions.
- Scan 006, R6, cancelled sign before x-sub-i-minus-1: The delta candidate remains locally cancelled before x_{i−1}, while the surviving displayed term is −e₁x_{i−1}. Both the deletion state and the index are preserved.
- Scan 006, R6, second inequality sign and unfinished range: The written condition is the open chain 2≤i≤. H keeps only 2≤i. No upper bound is inserted after the second sign.
- Scan 006, R6, descending equality: The vertical equals retains the connection between the two written rows beneath the left member; no additional horizontal chain is supplied.
- Scan 006, R6, predecessor overwritten by first coefficient s: The S candidate remains locally cancelled before live s, preserving both local writing states inside the globally cancelled system.
- Scan 006, R6, first live coefficient: The lower left member reads delta(s e_{i+1}+t f_{i+1}); H writes alpha at the first coefficient. The right member also contains s e_i. No equality s=alpha is supplied.
- Scan 006, R6, second coefficient: The lower left member has t before f_{i+1}, and the right member has t f_i. H writes beta at the left. The record does not assign t=beta.
- Scan 006, R6, right-hand side of final equation: The legible right member s e_i+t f_i replaces H’s ill marker. Both coefficients and indices are preserved. The four recorded checks do not verify this delta-system equation.
- Scan 017, Third equality: first join physical cluster: The first written join is resolved to the retraced cup; C had left cup/amalg open. This is a formula-reading resolution, with no check of the asserted identity.
